## Protocols


<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `GuardPipelineProtocol`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Protocols' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-contracts/src/lexigram/contracts/ai/guards.py#L127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Protocol for guard pipeline execution.

Orchestrates a chain of input and/or output guards, collecting
results and determining the aggregate action.

Protocol for guard evaluation results.

Every guard returns a result indicating whether the content
passed, was blocked, or triggered a warning.

### `InputGuardProtocol`

Protocol for input content guards.

Input guards inspect user prompts and messages before they are
sent to an LLM.  They can block, warn, or redact content.

Protocol for output content guards.

Output guards inspect LLM responses before they are returned
to the caller.  They can block, warn, or redact content.

Base class for all input content guards.

Subclasses implement check to evaluate a piece of input
content and return a <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-guard/api/#guardcheckresult' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>GuardCheckResult</a>.

Base class for all output content guards.

Subclasses implement check to evaluate LLM response content
and return a <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-guard/api/#guardcheckresult' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>GuardCheckResult</a>.

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `AggregateGuardResult`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-guard/src/lexigram/ai/guard/pipeline/result.py#L141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Aggregate result from running multiple guards in a pipeline.

Combines results from all guards into a single verdict.
The aggregate action is the most severe action from any guard
(BLOCK > REDACT > WARN > PASS).

Build an aggregate result from a list of individual results.

The aggregate action uses the most severe outcome. If multiple
guards redacted content, only the last redaction is applied (guards
should be ordered most-restrictive-first in the pipeline).

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `GuardConfig`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-guard/src/lexigram/ai/guard/config.py#L14' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Configuration for the content safety guard pipeline.

Controls which guards are enabled by default and their behaviour.
Guards can be further customised programmatically via
<a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-guard/api/#guardpipeline' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>GuardPipeline</a>.

Attributes:
    enabled: Master switch — when ``False`` the guard pipeline is
             bypassed entirely.
    injection_detection: Enable the prompt injection detector.
    injection_action: Action on detected injection (``"block"``/``"warn"``).
    pii_detection: Enable PII detection on inputs.
    pii_action: Action on detected PII (``"redact"``/``"block"``/``"warn"``).
    pii_entities: PII entity types to detect.  Empty list means all types.
    pii_redaction_output: Enable PII redaction on LLM outputs.
    max_input_chars: Maximum allowed input length in characters.
                     ``0`` disables the length guard.
    max_output_chars: Maximum allowed output length in characters.
                      ``0`` disables the output length guard.
    length_action: Action when length limit exceeded (``"block"``/``"warn"``).
    restricted_topics: Topics to block in user inputs.

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-guard/src/lexigram/ai/guard/module.py#L15' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Module for content safety guard pipeline.

Registers the <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-guard/api/#guardprovider' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>GuardProvider</a>
which builds and wires the configured guard pipeline into the container.

Usage

```python
app = LexigramApplication(
    modules=[..., GuardModule()],
)
```


    app = LexigramApplication(
        modules=[..., GuardModule()],
    )

Or with custom config

```python
app = LexigramApplication(
    modules=[
        ...,
        GuardModule(
            config=GuardConfig(
                injection_detection=True,
                pii_action="redact",
                max_input_chars=8000,
            )
        )
    ]
)
```


    app = LexigramApplication(
        modules=[
            ...,
            GuardModule(
                config=GuardConfig(
                    injection_detection=True,
                    pii_action="redact",
                    max_input_chars=8000,
                )
            )
        ]
    )

Create a GuardModule suitable for unit and integration testing.

Uses a pass-through (allow-all) guard pipeline with no external API
calls. Audit logging is disabled by default to keep test output clean.

Ordered chain of input and output guards.

Guards are evaluated in order.  For input, content flows through
each guard; if a guard redacts content, the redacted version is
passed to subsequent guards.  A BLOCK stops evaluation immediately.



<div style='margin:0;line-height:1.4;'><span style='display:block;font-size:0.7em;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.07em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--color-brand);margin-top:1rem;margin-bottom:0.4rem;'>Parameters</span><table style='border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:0.85em;margin:0;margin-bottom:1rem;table-layout:fixed;'><thead><tr><th style='text-align:left;padding:0.4rem var(--color-border-weak);width:55%;'>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem;vertical-align:top;white-space:nowrap;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);font-size:0.85em;color:var(--lex-color-name);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>`input_guards`</td><td var(--color-border-weak);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>Guards to run on user inputs before sending to LLM.</td></tr><tr><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem;vertical-align:top;white-space:nowrap;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);font-size:0.85em;color:var(--lex-color-name);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>`output_guards`</td><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem 0.6rem 1.2rem;vertical-align:top;font-size:0.9em;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);color:var(--color-text-weak);border-left:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>Guards to run on LLM outputs before returning to caller.</td></tr></tbody></table></div>


Example

```python
pipeline = GuardPipeline(
    input_guards=[
        PromptInjectionDetector(action="block"),
        PIIDetector(action="redact"),
    ],
    output_guards=[
        PIIRedactor(entities=["SSN", "CREDIT_CARD"]),
        LengthGuard(max_chars=10000, action="block"),
    ],
)

result = await pipeline.check_input(user_message)
if result.blocked:
    return Err(GuardViolationError(result))
safe_input = result.final_content or user_message
```


    pipeline = GuardPipeline(
        input_guards=[
            PromptInjectionDetector(action="block"),
            PIIDetector(action="redact"),
        ],
        output_guards=[
            PIIRedactor(entities=["SSN", "CREDIT_CARD"]),
            LengthGuard(max_chars=10000, action="block"),
        ],
    )

    result = await pipeline.check_input(user_message)
    if result.blocked:
        return Err(GuardViolationError(result))
    safe_input = result.final_content or user_message

Run all input guards against the content.

Guards are applied in order.  Redacted content is forwarded to
subsequent guards.  A BLOCK terminates evaluation immediately.
If `parallel` is True, all guards are run concurrently and redaction
chaining is disabled (each operates on the original content).

### `GuardPipelineCompletedHook`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `GuardProvider`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-guard/src/lexigram/ai/guard/di/provider.py#L35' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Provider for the content safety guard pipeline.

Reads <a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-guard/api/#guardconfig' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>GuardConfig</a>, builds a
<a href='/platform/ai/lexigram-ai-guard/api/#guardpipeline' style='color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(128,128,128,0.3);text-underline-offset:2px;'>GuardPipeline</a>
with the configured guards, and registers it as a singleton.

When ``GuardConfig.enabled`` is ``False``, a no-op pipeline with no
guards is registered (all checks pass through).

When ``GuardConfig.enable_llm_guards`` is ``True`` and an
``LLMClientProtocol`` is available in the container (resolved
optionally during boot), LLM-based injection and jailbreak
detectors are appended to the pipeline.

Boot phase — optionally attach LLM-based guards if configured.

Resolves ``LLMClientProtocol`` from the container when
``GuardConfig.enable_llm_guards`` is ``True``.  If the client is
not registered, LLM guards are silently skipped.

Shutdown phase — no cleanup required for guard pipeline.

Health check — always healthy (in-process domain provider).

No external backend to ping.

GuardProtocol that enforces a maximum input length.

Length is measured in character count.  For a rough token
estimate divide by 4 (GPT tokenizer average).

Example

```python
guard = InputLengthGuard(max_chars=8000, action="block")
result = await guard.check(user_input)
if not result.passed:
    return Err(InputTooLongError(len(user_input)))
```


    guard = InputLengthGuard(max_chars=8000, action="block")
    result = await guard.check(user_input)
    if not result.passed:
        return Err(InputTooLongError(len(user_input)))

Example

```python
guard = OutputLengthGuard(max_chars=50000, action="warn")
result = await guard.check(llm_response)
```

### `PIIDetector`

Example

```python
guard = PIIDetector(action="redact", entities=["EMAIL", "SSN"])
result = await guard.check(user_message)
safe_content = result.redacted_content or user_message
```


    guard = PIIDetector(action="redact", entities=["EMAIL", "SSN"])
    result = await guard.check(user_message)
    safe_content = result.redacted_content or user_message

Output guard that redacts PII from LLM responses.



<div style='margin:0;line-height:1.4;'><span style='display:block;font-size:0.7em;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.07em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--color-brand);margin-top:1rem;margin-bottom:0.4rem;'>Parameters</span><table style='border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:0.85em;margin:0;margin-bottom:1rem;table-layout:fixed;'><thead><tr><th style='text-align:left;padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;color:var(--color-text-strong);font-weight:600;font-size:0.82em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);width:20%;'>Parameter</th><th style='text-align:left;padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;color:var(--color-text-strong);font-weight:600;font-size:0.82em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);width:25%;'>Type</th><th style='text-align:left;padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;color:var(--color-text-strong);font-weight:600;font-size:0.82em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);padding-left:1.2rem;border-left:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);width:55%;'>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem;vertical-align:top;white-space:nowrap;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);font-size:0.85em;color:var(--lex-color-name);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>`entities`</td><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem;vertical-align:top;color:var(--lex-color-type) !important;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);font-size:0.82em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'></td><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem 0.6rem 1.2rem;vertical-align:top;font-size:0.9em;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);color:var(--color-text-weak);border-left:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>List of PII entity types to redact. Defaults to all supported types.</td></tr><tr><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem;vertical-align:top;white-space:nowrap;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);font-size:0.85em;color:var(--lex-color-name);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>`action`</td><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem;vertical-align:top;color:var(--lex-color-type) !important;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);font-size:0.82em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'></td><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem 0.6rem 1.2rem;vertical-align:top;font-size:0.9em;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);color:var(--color-text-weak);border-left:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>``"redact"`` (default) or ``"block"`` (rejects any response containing PII rather than sanitising it).</td></tr></tbody></table></div>


Example

```python
guard = PIIRedactor(entities=["SSN", "CREDIT_CARD", "EMAIL"])
result = await guard.check(llm_response)
safe_response = result.final_content or llm_response
```


    guard = PIIRedactor(entities=["SSN", "CREDIT_CARD", "EMAIL"])
    result = await guard.check(llm_response)
    safe_response = result.final_content or llm_response

<div data-pagefind-weight='10'>

### `PromptInjectionDetector`

</div>

<span data-api-type='Classes' style='display:none;'></span>

<div style='display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:-0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem;'><a href='https://github.com/dbtinoy-/lexigram/blob/main/lexigram-ai-guard/src/lexigram/ai/guard/input/injection.py#L104' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer' style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:0.3rem;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--sl-color-gray-3);text-decoration:none;'><svg viewBox='0 0 16 16' width='14' height='14' fill='currentColor'><path d='M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8c0 3.54 2.29 6.53 5.47 7.59.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82.64-.18 1.32-.27 2-.27.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8.013 8.013 0 0016 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8z'/></svg>source</a></div>
Heuristic detector for prompt injection and jailbreak attempts.

Blocks or warns on content that attempts to override system
instructions, break out of a persona, or exfiltrate the system prompt.



<div style='margin:0;line-height:1.4;'><span style='display:block;font-size:0.7em;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.07em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--color-brand);margin-top:1rem;margin-bottom:0.4rem;'>Parameters</span><table style='border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:0.85em;margin:0;margin-bottom:1rem;table-layout:fixed;'><thead><tr><th style='text-align:left;padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;color:var(--color-text-strong);font-weight:600;font-size:0.82em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);width:20%;'>Parameter</th><th style='text-align:left;padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;color:var(--color-text-strong);font-weight:600;font-size:0.82em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);width:25%;'>Type</th><th style='text-align:left;padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;color:var(--color-text-strong);font-weight:600;font-size:0.82em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);padding-left:1.2rem;border-left:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);width:55%;'>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem;vertical-align:top;white-space:nowrap;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);font-size:0.85em;color:var(--lex-color-name);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>`action`</td><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem;vertical-align:top;color:var(--lex-color-type) !important;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);font-size:0.82em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'></td><td style='padding:0.6rem 0.5rem 0.6rem 1.2rem;vertical-align:top;font-size:0.9em;font-family:var(--sl-font-mono);color:var(--color-text-weak);border-left:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);border-bottom:1px solid var(--color-border-weak);'>Action when injection is detected — ``"block"`` (default) or ``"warn"``.</td></tr></tbody></table></div>


Example

```python
guard = PromptInjectionDetector(action="block")
result = await guard.check(user_message)
if not result.passed:
    return Err(InjectionAttemptError())
```


    guard = PromptInjectionDetector(action="block")
    result = await guard.check(user_message)
    if not result.passed:
        return Err(InjectionAttemptError())

### `TopicRestrictor`

positives.</td></tr></tbody></table></div>


Example

```python
guard = TopicRestrictor(
    restricted_topics=["weapons", "explosives", "self-harm"],
    action="block",
)
result = await guard.check(user_message)
```


    guard = TopicRestrictor(
        restricted_topics=["weapons", "explosives", "self-harm"],
        action="block",
    )
    result = await guard.check(user_message)

Apply input and/or output safety guards to the decorated async function.

Guards are attached as metadata on the wrapper. The actual guard pipeline
execution is handled by the GuardPipelineProtocol implementation resolved
from the container — this decorator is a marker/configuration mechanism.

**Example**

@guarded(
    input_guards=[PromptInjectionGuard()],
    output_guards=[PIIFilterGuard()],
)
async def chat(prompt: str) -> str: ...
